AI 估值疑慮引發波動，惟股市仍有多頭可期
2026 年 7 月
全球主要股市 6 月 （截至24 日） 報酬跌多漲少，與5 月新興亞洲及 AI 半導體題材主導下的多頭
格局形成鮮明對比，主要因為市場同時承受美伊衝突升溫、 Fed 新主席華許上任後鷹派轉向，以及
AI 類股估值偏高引發的修正壓力，風險資產動能熄火。以美元計價6 月表現觀察，MSCI 中國與拉丁
美洲指數分別下跌 5.9%及 4.5%，表現最差。中國主因房市持續疲弱與內需復甦不均，並受其對中東
原油進口的高度依賴拖累 ， 拉丁美洲則受前波累積漲幅較大後的獲利了結與美元期間走強雙重壓抑。

美股因 AI 估值疑慮再起而拉回，拖累MSCI 新興市場與新興亞洲分別下跌 1.3%。惟有 MSCI 印度逆
勢上漲 1.9%，主要受惠 6 月下旬國際油價回落帶動。整體而言，6 月股市回檔較像是高基期後的短
線修正，並未改變今年資金偏好新興亞洲、日本與科技成長市場的方向。
6 月（截至 24 日）全球債市呈現「利率類承壓、信用類抗跌」的結構性分歧。全球主權債券單
月下跌 1.03%，跌幅居首，主因華許於 6 月會議釋出鷹派訊號，18 位官員中有 9 位於點陣圖計入今
年內至少一次升息，使美國 10 年期公債殖利率一度升至 4.5%，存續期間較長之利率類債券遭逢重
估。全球投資級公司債下跌 0.67%，跌幅次之，亦受存續期間拖累。新興當地債小跌 0.12%，反映
因美元指數回升使來自匯率的貢獻由正轉負。信用類債券則表現相對 較佳，新興美元債券單月上漲
0.53%、亞洲高收益債上漲 0.49%領先，主要受惠信用利差續處歷史低檔、企業基本面強健且違約率
仍偏低、到期牆延後且供給以再融資為主。

近期股市雖因 Fed 升息疑慮及 AI 類股估值過高而修正，但美光財報及蘋果商品漲價均印證 AI
產品供需緊俏且企業資本支出依然強勁 。 市場近期以類股輪動方式進行修正 ， 顯示投資人對於整體股
市信心仍在。在投資策略上，短期不宜追逐高估值個股，但可於震盪時分批布局 AI 基礎建設、半導
體供應鏈及台日股市。
債市方面，當前美債提供高於過往均值的高殖利率，將可相當程度抵銷殖利率續揚的風險。然因
投資人對 Fed 後續貨幣政策存有疑慮，或導致殖利率曲線短端受升息預期而有較大波動；長端又有
期限溢酬偏高、政府財政惡化及公債供給增加等風險。因此，建議債券佈局可聚焦 3 至 10 年期美國
公債及投等債，不僅能提供相對具吸引力的息收，若未來通膨預期放緩，在資本利得亦有表現機會。

美國股市：企業盈餘亮點集中 AI，股市評價尚屬合理，經濟成長趨緩但韌性猶存
美股第一季財報顯示，標普 500 成分股獲利有八成企業超越市場預期，整體每股盈餘同比增長
26.2%，創下 2021 年以來新高，企業盈餘上修動能強勁。隨運算需求從大模型訓練轉向推論，市場
資金快速轉向記憶體、光通訊、ASIC、CPU 與通用型伺服器供應鏈，引領板塊快速輪動。接下來第
二季 AI 產業仍將帶動資訊業獲利高增長，而能源、原物料產業第二季財報仍有不錯表現，但消費、
金融、醫療等，甚至通訊服務產業都有成長放緩跡象。
美國關稅談判正轉向以「301 法案」推進，目前針對「強迫勞動貨品進口禁令」，美國貿易代表

署（USTR） 已建議對全球60 個經濟體加徵 10%與 12.5%的額外關稅 。 預料下半年陸續將有更多301
調查結果公布，這將為川普關稅談判提供法源依據，迫使各國供應鏈加速赴美設廠，為美國製造業注
入投資動能，故對美國整體經濟表現無須看淡。
伴隨企業獲利預期上修 ， 標普500 指數前瞻本益比目前仍在 21.45 倍水準 ， 且通訊服務僅在20.9
倍、資訊科技僅在 24.8 倍，估值水平依舊合理。當前市場質疑重點在於AI 產業吸引大量資金投入資
本支出，故縮減企業發放現金股利與股票回購的能力，然而考量下半年仍有期中選舉行情可期，且
AI 產業變革快速，新題材層出不窮，操作上維持分批布局的建議。
美國經濟在 2026 年表現顯示成長趨緩但韌性猶存，2026 年第一季 GDP 成長率為 2.6%，表現
仍優於歐元區、日本等其他市場。失業率維持在低檔，5 月非農新增就業仍有 17.2 萬人，失業率持
平於 4.3%。5 月 CPI 年增率升至 4.3%， 核心CPI 年增率加速到 2.9%， 能源價格仍為推升通膨主因；

同月份 PPI 與核心 PPI 分別來到 6.5%與 4.9%， 顯示上游成本壓力正持續向下游轉嫁。勞動市場結構
性分歧，金融就業人數下滑，然而資料中心、半導體與電力等基礎建設投資，拉動非住宅營建業與製
造業僱傭需求，支撐整體就業數據，消費端方面，5 月美國零售銷售月增率回升至 0.9%，其中加油
站銷售與無店鋪零售商銷售(電商)表現強勁，前月走弱的汽車及零部件銷售逆勢反彈，顯示在K 型經
濟支撐下，高收入群體與特定消費仍表現穩健。
預料新任主席華許在確立聯準會改革方案前不急於升息，但目前利率仍處歷史高位，對房市、汽
車和消費造成壓抑 。 觀察密西根大學消費者信心指數在第二季來到有紀錄以來最低 ， 且美國新屋開工
下降，房市持續低迷，美國經濟仍需消化高利率與通膨帶來的負面效應。

歐洲股市：歐洲央行態度轉鷹，靜待美伊停火與關稅明朗化
歐洲央行在 6 月會議升息 1 碼，並持續警告中東地緣政治風險可能為通膨前景增添不確定性，因
此將今年通膨預期上調至 3.0%（前值 2.6%） ， 預計要到明年下半年才有機會回到 2% 的政策目標 。
不過，與 2022 年通膨高峰時期不同，目前歐洲就業市場並未出現明顯過熱，薪資壓力也未持續升
溫，因此我們認為本次升息後，歐洲央行更可能轉向觀望，利率期貨則顯示，市場仍預期年底前有再
升息 1 碼的可能性。
歐洲央行本次會議將邊際貸款利率、主要再融資利率及存款機制利率上調 1 碼至 2.65% 、

2.4% 與 2.25% ，會議中所有委員均同意升息。勞動力需求進一步降溫，薪資成長預計將在一年內
放緩，目前沒有看到與工資相關的二次效應，本次會議未討論中性利率和中性區間，如果不升息，通
膨因此失控 ， 重新控制將更加困難 。GDP 成長預估來看 ，2026 年爲 0.8%，2027 年爲 1.2%，2028
年爲 1.5% 。通膨方面，2026 年爲 3%，2027 年爲 2.3%，2028 年爲 2.0% 。核心通膨來看，
2026 年爲 2.5%，2027 年爲 2.5%，2028 年爲 2.2% 。
歐洲經濟成長動能放緩，但製造業維持擴張態勢，雖然歐洲央行將 2026、2027 年經濟成長率預
估值下調，自 0.9%、1.3%調整至 0.8%、1.2%。然 6 月歐元區綜合 PMI 自 48.5 回升至 49.5，顯示
景氣萎縮幅度正逐步收窄，製造業活動仍維持在擴張區間，同月服務業 PMI 雖僅 48.9，但也已釋出
跌勢趨緩訊號。
ECB 升息 1 碼對抗通膨風險，後續升息路徑分歧，受美伊衝突引發的油價影響，歐洲通膨壓力再

度上升，5 月歐元區 CPI 年增率已達 3.2%，為防範通膨失控，歐洲央行在 6 月利率決策會議將存款
利率從 2.0%上調至 2.25%。目前市場對歐洲央行後續的升息路徑看法顯著分歧，鷹派陣營認為 6 月
升息 1 碼後、第三季會再升 1 碼，並維持利率水平至 2027 年底；鴿派觀點認為隨油價回跌，短期內
再升息必要性降低，故後續仍將觀察各國通膨轉嫁能力。
待美伊戰事落幕、美歐關稅底定，歐股可望啟動估值修復，展望後市，若美伊停火協議落實、引
領國際油價回吐先前漲幅，將成為扭轉歐股當前弱勢、並吸引國際資金回流的催化劑。此外，美國改
採以 301 條款課徵關稅，對歐洲出口實質影響仍須觀察，惟預料美歐盟友關係穩固，終將透過談判
達成雙邊協議。目前泛歐 600 本益比以 2026 年獲利僅 15.6 倍。待短期不確定性因素陸續消退，市
場情緒可望改善，有利啟動估值修復行情。

日本市場：成長動能仰賴出口，政府補貼支撐景氣表現，股市屢創新高
GDP 動能放緩，受惠族群集中於AI，日本 2026 年第一季 GDP 年增率僅 0.4%，創下 2024 年走
出經濟衰退以來最疲弱表現，市場已將全年度 GDP 成長預估下調至 0.6%。然而 5 月出口年增來到
17%，創 2022 年 11 月以來最快增速，其中半導體相關出口年增逾六成、反映全球資料中心需求全
面爆發，推升記憶體晶片與電子零組件價格走揚，故受惠族群集中，下半年成長動能仍高度仰賴 AI
產業。
財政補貼壓低通膨 ， 油價回跌降低財政負擔，近期 ，首相高市早苗推行 「責任的積極財政」 政策，

透過實施電費、瓦斯補貼，並將全國汽油價格維持在每公升約170 日圓水平。未來隨補貼措施退場，
預期物價將出現階段性反彈。日本央行已於利率決策會議把本年度核心通膨預估上調至 2.8%。然而
在利率走高背景下，日本政府財政結構惡化，根據2026 財年政府預算規劃，國債利息支出將達總預
算的 10.65%，明顯高於前一財年的 9.13%。所幸美伊戰事有降溫跡象，預期日本政府將持續縮減刺
激方案規模。
日銀升息 1 碼，美日利差未收斂壓抑日圓表現，且日本央行於 6 月會議決定升息 1 碼至 1.0%，
主要考量國內物價上行風險加劇、薪資維持強勁成長動能，且經濟下行風險受控。市場普遍預期下次
升息落在 2026 年第四季。然而高市若為緩解財政壓力，可能干預日銀升息路徑。鑑於美日利差未出
現明顯收斂 ， 即使日本官方進場干預 ， 對日圓匯率的支撐效果恐怕相對有限 ， 日圓匯率預估於155-163
區間震盪。
日經指數近期屢創新高，6 月 22 日史上首度突破 72,000 點、連七個交易日刷新紀錄。上漲動能

來自日圓貶至 161 附近，顯著優化出口企業的盈餘表現，同時「實體 AI」投資計畫引爆半導體與 AI
類股行情 。 就2026 年以來 ， 領漲標的涵蓋東京威力科創 、 村田製作所 、 鎧俠 、 軟銀、 三菱UFJ 金融、
愛德萬測試、松下控股、三井住友金融、瑞穗金融、藤倉等。上漲族群主要為 AI 供應鏈以及受惠於
升息的金融族群，但即使過去一年日股飆升，目前以 2026 年獲利，本益比仍在 24 倍合理位置。
波克夏深化日本投資佈局，外資買盤同步加碼，波克夏對日本資產配置持續增加。自 2020 年對
伊藤忠、丸紅、三菱商事、三井物產、住友商事等五家各以略高於 5%持股起步，至 2026 年 5 月對
各家持股均已突破 10%， 顯示加碼日本仍是其核心策略 。 與此同時 ， 外資對日股買盤持續湧入 ，2026
年迄今(截至 6 月 20 日為止)外資買超日股的累計金額擴大至 10.2 兆日圓 ， 反映國際資本對日股後勢
依舊樂觀。

台灣股市：AI 需求支撐製造業 PMI 高檔擴張，暑假旺季與股市推升服務動能
當前臺灣經濟正處於生產擴張的黃金期。最新公布的總體經濟數據與半導體龍頭營收呈現全面共
振，印證全球 AI 資本支出（CapEx）並非流於故事題材，而是實質轉化為台廠的訂單與獲利。儘管
全球金融市場短期受到地緣政治干擾與美債殖利率波動影響 ， 引發台股在高檔震盪 ， 但主計總處大幅
上修 2026 年 GDP 預測至 9.64%，配合製造業 PMI 續擴張至 60.7%，顯示基本面強勁。我們認
為，台股高檔的回檔屬於健康的獲利了結與籌碼沉澱，長線多頭架構未變。建議採取拉回布局策略，

聚焦 AI 實體基礎設施與邊緣運算（Edge AI）雙主線。
國發會公布之 6 月製造業 PMI 報 60.7， 連續第 6 個月維持在 60.0%以上的擴張速度 ， 六大產業
中，僅食品暨紡織產業與交通工具回報緊縮。細項中，新增訂單與生產分別上揚至 60.4（前 59.6）
與 58.7（前 57.9） ，同時原物料存貨和客戶存貨分別回跌至 61.9（前 65.7）和 49.4（前 50.5），
顯示在 AI 伺服器、半導體與高階製造的強勁拉貨下，庫存水位仍屬可控。
5 月海關出口達 784.8 億美元（年增 51.7%） ，寫下連 31 紅紀錄。其中，資通訊產品出口年
增 75.2%，電子零組件出口創歷史新高。同時，5 月進口額創下 605.7 億美元歷史新高（年增
54.9%） ，核心物資多為半導體資本設備，依進口領先出口 1 至 2 季之歷史慣例，預示下半年出貨
動能將再創高峰。台積電 5 月合併營收達 4,169 億 7,500 萬元（年增 30.1%） 。先進製程與高階
封裝產能持續滿載，且透過定價調整順利維持毛利率在高檔，讓獲利能見度直接延伸至後續數年。

主計總處正式將 2026 全年經濟成長率（GDP）預測大幅上修 1.93 個百分點至 9.64%，創下
16 年來新高紀錄。第一季 GDP 初估更達 14.55%，創 48 年來單季新高。 總經結構分析顯示，
這波榮景是由「出口拉動投資、投資進而促進消費」的良性循環。全球主要雲端服務供應商（CSP）
調高資本支出，其新一代伺服器建置「必須向台灣採購」 。商品出口實質成長預估將帶動全年商品出
口總值達 8,945 億美元，其年增率創 50 年來最高水準，構成台灣 GDP 增長的關鍵引擎。
儘管台股指數在高檔震盪加劇，但在 9.64% 的 GDP 強勁基本面保護下，下檔支撐極度紮實。
隨著 COMPUTEX 後終端裝置陸續開出 ， 消費性電子將迎來升級週期 ， 可望讓下半年經濟加速升溫。
我們持續看多台灣股市。

中國股市：製造業需求回溫，然動能僅集中於高新技術產業與出口端
中國 6 月製造業需求回溫，動能集中新產業與出口端，製造業 PMI 為 50.3（前 50.0） ，較前
月回升，並連續四個月維持在擴張區間。主要動能來自新出口訂單回升至 50.1（前 48.6） ，以及新
訂單升至 51.2（前 49.9） ，皆較前月回升至擴張區間，顯示需求端及出口端表現亦有所回溫。生產
經營活動預期上升至 54.3（前 53.9） ，製造業前景保有一定韌性，細項觀察，6 月生產指數 51.4
（前 51.2） ，持續維持在擴張區間，本月在新訂單回升、庫存指數回落至 47.7（前 49.3）之下，新

訂單減庫存擴大至 3.5（前 0.6） 。 另外受到美伊衝突緩解影響 ， 主要原材料價格大幅回落至 54.2（前
60.5） ，外部壓力緩解。
產業方面，新舊動能延續上月分化格局。高技術製造業 PMI 升至 53.5（前 52.9） ，裝備製造業
升至 52.5（前 52.1） ，上升趨勢持續，相較之下，傳統高耗能產業 47.1（前 47.1） ，建築業商業活
動 49.0（前 48.8） ，仍維持在較弱格局。整體來看，美伊衝突緩解有效帶動中國製造廠商轉趨樂觀，
但多數仍集中在新產業及出口端，後續仍待觀察新產業能否有效帶動內需動能回升。
然而，內需數據尚未見回溫：中國第二季以來的消費、固定投資數據下滑，房地產未見起色。工
業生產年增長 4.5%，表現最為堅挺，主要受到政策性資本開支與科技硬需求的支撐。零售銷售5 月
單月年增率轉負， 即便有五一黃金周的短暫拉動，但節後服務消費迅速墜落顯示出居民消費依然保
守。固定資產投資年比下降 4.1% 。 這是過往作為壓艙石的投資首次跌破 4% 防線。民間固定資

產投資年比更劇烈下滑 7.1%。
整體來說，中國政府大規模刺激的可能性偏低，主要政策料是調降 LPR 或結構性定向降準。其
政策核心在於為中下游民營中小企業減輕債務壓力。現行溫和的企業設備更新與家電 「以舊換新」 政
策邊際效益遞減。為遏制消費滑入通縮螺旋，中央財政下半年將擴大赤字，直接補貼居民端，如發放
消費券、加大育兒養老津貼等等。中國總體經濟正處於重組期，缺乏向上突破的動能，但向下有國家
政策工具與新興硬科技的實質托底 。 房地產全面反轉或大眾消費復甦的機率偏低 。 雖政府政策扶植的
新科技產業仍有機會，但整體看法仍偏向保守。

印度股市：外部供給衝擊與內部基本面的對抗，看法中性
全球供應鏈受到中東戰事的實質干擾 ， 印度的前瞻景氣指標卻給市場帶來了巨大的驚喜 。 最新
公布的 2026 年 5 月 HSBC 印度綜合採購經理人指數由 4 月的 58.2 逆勢回升至 59.3，創下近
6 個月來的最高擴張紀錄。其中，服務業 PMI 由 58.8 顯著彈升至 59.8，主因是國內消費模式在
中東局勢干擾下成功「外轉內」 ，內需服務動能十分強勁；製造業 PMI 5 月最終值亦來到 55.0，高
於 4 月的 54.7。 雖然新出口訂單因中東衝突擴大而減速 ， 但國內基礎建設與製造業的 「預防性囤貨」

需求，成功支撐製造業維持在歷史平均水準之上。
然而，內需並無法完全沖刷外部供給側帶來的窒息感。印度高達 85% 的原油依賴進口，中東
戰事導致國際油價攀升至每桶近 120 美元，對印度財政與物價造成極大考驗。最新數據顯示，5 月
份消費者物價指數（CPI）年增率由 4 月的 3.5% 抬頭至 3.9%。這主要是受到汽車燃油價格凍結
政策結束的影響 。此外 ，比原油更長遠的隱憂來自於氣候 。 氣象機構預警有高機率出現乾旱 。 歷史上，
降雨不足與聖嬰現象會嚴重抑制糧食與作物產量 ， 豆類、 油籽等作物的受災風險顯著高於有灌溉保護
的水稻和小麥。彭博與野村等機構一致預警，這項氣候乾旱威脅將在 10 月農作物收成期引爆新一
輪食品通膨，預估將食品與飲料通膨推向 6.0%，並使下半年整體 CPI 衝破央行的容忍上限。
面對經常帳赤字擴大、國際收支（BOP）惡化與盧比貶值壓力，印度當局並未坐以待斃。首先
在貨幣政策方面，印度央行（RBI）於 6 月 5 日一致決議維持政策回購利率在 5.25% 不變，立場

維持「中性」 。央行認為盲目升息防禦匯率不僅無效，反而可能將能源衝擊轉化為內需重傷，因此決
策者選擇保留政策空間以依賴數據靈活決策。不過，經濟學家多數預期，隨著年底通膨風險逐漸壓過
成長擔憂，央行這項「按兵不動」的政策不會持續太久。 其次在財政工具方面，為保障佔人口半數
的農業人口並穩定農村經濟，政府宣布 2026/27 財年肥料補貼預算大增 20%，並階段性減免汽柴
油，支撐內需經濟。
印度市場目前正呈現明顯的「基本面強韌」與「評價面修正」的矛盾訊號。不可否認，5 月高
企的 PMI 與 1Q GDP 的暴衝證實其內部需求並未崩潰，而政府靈活的黃金關稅政策亦成功控制了
經常帳的惡化 。 然而 ， 中東戰事帶來的原油窒息感與下半年季風降雨不足 ， 是極難迴避的供給側利空 。
這兩項毒素預計將在第四季將通膨推向 6% 以上，進而逼迫央行轉為年底升息，更高的借貸成本屆
時將進一步抑制工業與內需需求，也讓股市有評價修正的壓力。因此，我們建議現階段應採取較為防
守的中長線投資策略，以在估值向下修正的震盪期中，鎖定長期結構性獲利。

拉美市場：巴西選舉風險與通膨預期升溫，後市應保守看待
6 月以來拉美市場主軸已由單純降息利多，轉為政治風險、通膨預期與央行動態三方拉扯。巴西
總統大選民調顯示魯拉領先幅度擴大，疊加巴西通膨預期與 Selic 利率路徑連續上修，使市場開始重
新評估 2026 年政策與財政不確定性，也使整體拉美股市表現持續承壓。
選舉方面，最新公布的 Genial/Quaest 民調指出，魯拉對波索納洛的領先幅度再度擴大，第一輪
由前次 39%對 33%，轉為本次 39%對 29%，魯拉領先差距升至 10 個百分點；第二輪亦由前次魯拉
42%對波索納洛 41%，轉為本次 44%對 38%，領先 6 個百分點。由於魯拉為較不受資本市場所青睞

的候選人，其當選可能性上升，預期將不利於巴西股市表現。
另一方面 ， 通膨預期惡化也使巴西降息交易持續降溫 。截至 6 月 15 日的巴西央行市場調查指出，
2026 年巴西通膨預期由 5.11%上修 19 個基點至 5.30%，持續高於 4.50%通膨目標區間上緣。2027
至 2029 年通膨預期亦全數高於 3.0%目標，顯示市場對通膨回到央行目標區間信心不足。
受此影響，市場對巴西政策利率路徑也持續轉鷹，2026、2027、2028 年底 Selic 利率預期分別
升至 13.75%、12.00%、10.25%，較前次的 13.50%、11.50%、10.00%再度上修。財政面同樣削弱
政策可信度，2026 至 2028 年基本財政餘額預期仍為 GDP 的-0.50%、-0.40%、-0.24%，名目財政
赤字 2026 至 2029 年亦維持超過 GDP 的 7%，顯示巴西財政赤字問題持續惡化。
貨幣政策方面，巴西央行 6 月 17 日全票決議降息 25 個基點，將 Selic 利率降至 14.25%，表面

上延續降息循環 ， 但其聲明與前瞻指引缺乏清晰度 ， 使市場更難判斷央行是否會對通膨預期重新升溫
採取足夠強硬態度。巴西央行通膨預測亦同步上修，2026 年底整體通膨預測由 4.6%升至 5.2%，自
由價格通膨預測在 2026 年第四季由 4.5%升至 5.3%，在通膨壓力持續升高的狀況下，巴西短期內進
一步降息空間已相當有限。
巴西以外拉美央行亦呈現類似政策兩難，智利央行 6 月 16 日宣布維持政策利率 4.5%不變 ， 即使
其 2026 年 GDP 成長預測降至 1%至 1.75%，但仍因油價與地緣風險推升短期通膨而選擇觀望。
展望後市，拉美市場在巴西大選風險升溫、通膨預期持續攀升的背景下，後市股市上行空間料將
受限，尤其全球股市近期受到 AI 題材帶動，拉美市場在缺乏成長敘事下，表現料將難以跟上全球指
數表現，故我們認為目前對拉美市場應保守看待。

科技類股：Token 經濟學與邊緣 AI 外溢效應，帶動科技股進一步走揚
當前全球科技股正處於由 AI 算力基礎設施引導的「結構性重新定價」新起點。首先半導體產值
將正式跨越破兆美元大關，市場對於「AI 泡沫破裂」的擔憂缺乏實質數據支持。超大型雲端服務商
（CSP） 具備強韌的資產負債表與 EBITDA 創造力，大舉發債並非盲目燒錢。「Token 經濟學」 打通
了算力消耗與商業變現的阻礙 ， 這些都是科技股的中長線利多。 雖然美國貨幣政策走向可能引發短線
估值震盪，但基本面依然偏多。
根據 IDC 最新報告，全球半導體市場規模正迎來歷史性的結構性跨越，2026 年全球半導體總營

收預估將達到 1.29 兆美元 ， 較 2025 年增 52.8%。 其中 ， 單是資料中心半導體營收就將達到 4,771
億美元，預計到 2030 年將達 8,432 億美元，獨佔市場半壁江山。2026 年全球記憶體營收暴增至
5,947 億美元，其中 DRAM 營收跳升至 4,186 億美元，年增率高達 177%。主因是 CSP 業者為
了搶奪 HBM 與高階 DDR 產能，願意支付極高溢價，推升整體板塊毛利率。
輝達執行長黃仁勳定調的 「Token 經濟學」 ，將技術敘事轉化為可量化的財務獲利模型，其核心
論點為 「Compute is revenue（運算就是收入） ，AI 工廠生產的實體商品就是 Token。 而產生Token
的數量就可以與資本開支互相匹配，進而由算力推估出資本開支的回報率。在 1GW 功耗限制下，
Blackwell 世代年營收約 300 億美元；進入次世代 Vera Rubin 晶片架構後，因運算密度拉升，相
同功耗下的年營收預估將暴增 5 倍至 1,500 億美元 ， 提供 CSP 業者持續加大資本支出的實質回報

率支持。
此外，隨著集中式雲端算力的建置跨過臨界點，AI 算力紅利正加速向終端裝置外溢。智慧型手機
與 PC 本地執行 AI 推論的需求已迎來全面爆發，Edge AI 正為半導體市場創造出全新的高價值需
求類別。邊緣 AI 擺脫了過往智慧終端僅比較出貨量的殺價競爭，轉向「高附加價值」的規格升級溢
價。長期而言，隨著 AI 代理（Agentic AI）在終端裝置的滲透率攀升，半導體將迎來新一波結構性
定價與穩健的增長動能。
短期來看，由於通膨數據具備黏性，市場正重新評估聯準會 （Fed） 貨幣政策再度升息的可能性。
此流動性緊縮預期直接推升了美債無風險利率 ，可能對高估值的科技權值股造成本益比階段性震盪修
正。然而，科技巨頭極其強韌的實質獲利能力足以消化估值衝擊。2026 年半導體跨越 1.29 兆美元
產值已成剛需，CSP 業者 EBITDA 預估增長 13%，配合黃仁勳 Token 經濟學的變現邏輯，獲利增
長將有效消化高本益比，建議可持續加碼科技類股。

### Page 10

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
能源類股：荷莫茲海峽運量逐步恢復，油價料將承壓
受到美伊戰爭持續朝收斂方向前進影響，市場重新評估波斯灣出口恢復後的供給回補壓力；同
時，近期中國原油需求與進口動能明顯轉弱，使油市主軸由短線斷供風險，轉向中期供給過剩與需求
破壞，受此影響，6 月油價出現大幅回落，相關能源類股表現也出現承壓。
高盛報告指出，自美伊 6 月 17 日達成臨時協議後，荷莫茲海峽與波斯灣出口流量快速回升，中
東局勢於 6 月 27 日油輪襲擊後再度升溫前，波斯灣石油流量七日均值已達每日 1,800 萬桶，約為戰
前水準近 80%。其中荷莫茲海峽流量在協議後前 10 日增加每日 700 萬桶，6 月 24 日單日估算流量

一度達每日 1,300 萬桶 ， 約為正常水準60%； 若依過去兩週平均每日增加100 萬桶的恢復速度推估，
波斯灣流量最快可在 7 月初回到戰前每日 2,300 萬桶水準。不過，6 月 27 日油輪襲擊後，多艘超大
型油輪傳出掉頭返航 ，6 月 27 日至 6 月 28 日前後經荷莫茲海峽出口量一度降至每日 500 萬桶以下 ，
不到正常水準 20%， 顯示中東石油出口恢復方向雖明確 ， 但短期內仍取決於船東對海峽安全的信心。
另一方面，需求端壓力也有望加速全球石油供需平衡。IEA（國際能源署）6 月月報估計，第二
季全球石油供應缺口為每日 310 萬桶 ， 低於高盛估計的每日 500 萬桶 ， 主因 IEA 對石油需求破壞
的估計更高，IEA 預估第二季需求破壞量為每日 550 萬桶，高於高盛估計的每日 490 萬桶。需求
端疲弱原因主要來自中國需求下滑。彭博分析指出，中國 5 月原油需求急劇下降，原油進口量降至
每日 680 萬桶，較去年同期下滑 40%，主因包含荷莫茲海峽關閉期間造成的採購干擾，以及經濟

放緩帶來的需求破壞。進一步看至 2027 年，IEA 預估在供給年增每日 790 萬桶，以及部分需求疲
弱延續下，全球每日將出現 500 萬桶供給過剩，高於高盛預估的每日 320 萬桶，顯示中期油市風
險已由短缺轉向過剩。
受到整體油市轉往供給過剩影響，高盛將 2026 年第四季布蘭特原油價格預測由每桶 90 美元下
修至 80 美元，2027 年平均價格由 80 美元下修至 75 美元；西德州原油方面，2026 年第四季均價
預估由 85 美元下修至 75 美元，2027 年則由 75 美元下修至 70 美元。摩根大通、巴克萊、摩根士
丹利、瑞銀與滙豐也紛紛下修今明兩年油價展望，預估 2027 年油價均價介於每桶 63 至 85 美元；
其中巴克萊預期第四季可能出現每日 300 萬桶供應過剩，滙豐則預期短期將出現小型供應過剩。
展望後市，能源類股仍需觀察美伊臨時備忘錄落實狀況、荷莫茲海峽運輸恢復及中東增產狀況是
否順暢，然而當前市場更為關注停戰帶來的風險溢價收斂，故中短期內油價表現料將較為疲軟，在此

狀況下 ， 能源類股表現料將難以勝過由 AI 題材帶動的全球指數表現 ，故對後市我們亦維持保守看法。

### Page 11

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
黃金類股：央行增持黃金趨勢未變，惟聯準會偏鷹不利金價表現
6 月以來黃金類股表現持續落後整體指數表現，主因受到美國聯準會態度偏鷹派影響，市場升息
預期上升，推升美元與美債殖利率走強，連帶提高黃金這類無孳息資產的機會成本，使金價與黃金類
股短線表現持續承壓。
6 月 FOMC 會議雖維持聯邦基準利率在 3.5%至 3.75%不變，但利率點陣圖中位數由 3 月時預估
2026 年底的 3.375%上修至 3.75%，同時 18 位提交預測委員中，有 9 位預期年內至少升息一次，8
位預期維持不變，僅 1 位預期降息 1 碼，導致會後市場升息預期明顯攀升。對黃金而言，升息預期

升溫將推升美元與美債殖利率 ， 並提高持有無息資產的機會成本 ， 尤其金價先前在美伊戰爭導致長天
期公債殖利率攀升的影響下，表現已相對疲弱，近期利率預期轉鷹更容易使短線獲利了結壓力升高。
另一方面，新任聯準會主席 Kevin Warsh 在本次會議中並未屈從政治面的降息壓力，反而重申
2%通膨目標並強調貨幣政策的獨立性，有助於強化市場對聯準會政策紀律的信心。此舉雖有助於提
升美國政策可信度，卻也將對黃金帶來不利影響；由於黃金的部分避險買盤，源自於市場對美國財政
負擔、政策獨立性及美元信用的疑慮，故一旦市場相信聯準會將維持堅定對抗通膨的立場，這類避險
需求便可能逐步減弱，也讓黃金類股失去美國政策性避險這項題材。
然而，儘管短線黃金逆風重重，但是在央行買盤方面，仍未見到全球央行對於增持黃金的看法有
所轉變。世界黃金協會所出版的《2026 年央行黃金儲備調查》顯示，89%受訪央行預期未來 12 個
月全球央行黃金儲備將增加 ， 雖低於2025 年的 95%， 但仍高於2024 年的 81%與 2023 年的 71%，

顯示多數國家央行對於持續增持黃金儲備仍具高度共識。就自身配置而言， 45%受訪央行預期未來
12 個月自身黃金儲備將增加，高於 2025 年的 43%與 2024 年的 29%，並創下 2019 年以來最高比
例，另有 54%預期維持不變，僅 1%預期下降，顯示各國央行黃金配置意願尚未出現明確轉向 態勢。
該項調查也指出，各國央行增持黃金的原因，主要仍集中在儲備多元化與風險分散。90%受訪央
行認為黃金在危機時期具有避險性 ，84%認為黃金具長期保值與通膨避險功能 ，83%認為黃金是有效
的投資組合分散工具，80%認為黃金屬於儲備多元化政策的一部分。由此可見，央行買金並非單純追
逐短線價格，而是與官方儲備安全性、幣別分散及危機應對需求有關。
展望後市，全球央行增持黃金的趨勢仍未改變，然而短線上，在降息利多消失、美元及美債殖利
率偏向上行，且聯準會獨立性疑慮降溫的背景下，金價表現料將受到壓抑，預期後市黃金類股表現將
於區間內震盪整理，目前對黃金類股後市中性看待。

### Page 12

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
固定收益：市場觀望華許首秀，美債利率料陷整理走勢
6 月中的 FOMC 會議以 12–0 一致決議維持聯邦資金利率目標區間於 3.50%–3.75%， 並重申維
持充足準備金政策。然而，投資人所真正關注的內容在於會後所公布的經濟預測摘要(SEP)。在 6 月
點陣圖中，九位官員預計今年至少會升息 1 碼，其中六位預計至少升息 2 次。另有九位官員預計利
率維持不變或會降息 。2026 年底聯邦基金利率中位數由 3 月的 3.4% 上修到 3.8%，2027 年由 3.1%
上修到 3.6%；同時，2026 年 PCE 通膨預估由 2.7% 大幅上修至 3.6%，核心 PCE 由 2.7% 上修

至 3.3%。這代表 Fed 不只承認通膨反彈，也認為利率路徑需要重新往上定價。彭博對於 Fed 主席
開場談話的 NLP 情緒模型亦反映了鷹派傾向的變化。
華許在此次的會後記者會中雖重申聯準會對物價穩定與最大化就業雙重使命的承諾 ， 但針對已持
續五年多高於 2%目標的通膨問題，他強調委員會將毫不含糊地致力於恢復物價穩定。同時Fed 也將
維持銀行系統的充足準備金政策。他也指出，儘管面臨不確定性，目前經濟活動穩步擴張，生產力和
資本投資強勁，且就業市場保持穩定；本次會議發布了更簡短直接的政策聲明，取消了華許認為不合
時宜的 「前瞻性指引」 ，使得此次會後聲明用字大幅精簡 （由4/29 會議時的 341 字減至 130 字） ，
Warsh 本人也選擇不參與提交經濟預測摘要（SEP）。作為領導層上任後的重要革新，他也宣布在未
來兩週內正式成立五個獨立小組以分別對「溝通」、「資產負債表」、「數據來源」、「生產力／就
業」與「通膨框架」等議題進行改革，旨在打造一個適應時代需求且專注於未來的聯準會。

美國總體經濟環境現處於「經濟具韌性但通膨僵固」的微妙平衡。即便 FOMC 6 月會議立場偏
向鷹派，但仍約有半數委員認為年底前利率將維持現行水準。彭博指出，倘美伊停火協議得以維持，
通膨或於未來數月回落，部分 6 月預期升息的委員 9 月可望轉為按兵不動。元大投顧認為「年底前
維持利率不變仍是大概率事件」 ，因近期震盪部分是因市場「尚未適應 Warsh 新溝通風格」所產生
的過度反應。此外，高盛在 FOMC 會後發表的最新報告也指出即便  Fed「升息的可能性雖增加，
但仍非其基本預期」。
我們認為，當前美債已充分反映通膨上行風險，並提供高於過往均值的高殖利率，將可相當程度
抵銷殖利率進一步走高之風險。然因投資人對 Fed 的後續貨幣政策存有疑慮，或導致殖利率曲線短
端受升息預期變化而有較大波動；長端又有期限溢酬偏高、政府財政惡化及公債供給增加等風險，故
存續期過長亦非明智之舉。因此，建議債券佈局可聚焦 3 至 10 年期美國公債及投等債，不僅能提供
相對具吸引力的息收，若未來通膨預期隨中東緊張情勢放緩，在資本利得方面亦可望有所表現。

### Page 3

Unimicron (3037.TW)
28 October 2026 Citi Research
3
Long-term strategies: Resources concentrated on ABF/ AI PCB — As we
highlighted, Unimicron has been converting BT capacity into ABF, which currently
causes some BT capacity to be idle during the transition period (negative impact will
likely last into 1H27, in our view). Thus, currently, we are less bullish on its BT GM
recovery in the near term vs other BT peers. However, we expect this conversion will

help the company to secure more ABF demand earlier considering customers
currently are still waiting for those ABF greenfield capacity expansion projects to
come in the next few years. For HDI/mSAP , Unimicron would only focus on AI
server/optical module products in the long term. To note that, the company is still
less aggressive in HDI capacity expansion given higher competition from Chinese
peers. Meanwhile, the company is also downsizing its traditional PCB capacity (i.e.,

for non-AI business) and just announced its plan to sell its PCB plant in Japan to
other players. We expect there will be more downsizing plans to come for traditional
PCB capacity.
GM expansion on track; reiterate Buy — We raise our 2026/27 /28E by 10%/5%/5%
to factor in robust GM expansion by ABF. We remain positive on its GM expansion by
ABF and also see potential upside coming from HDI and BT segments. We raise our
DCF-based TP to NT$1,550 (30x 2027E EPS) from NT$1,500 and reiterate our Buy

rating.

### Page 6

Unimicron (3037.TW)
28 October 2026 Citi Research
6
Unimicron
Company description
Established in 1990, Unimicron is the world's No.1 PCB maker and HDI board
maker. After the acquisition of PPT in late 2009, its revenue exposure to
substrate has surpassed 40%, with the rest almost equally shared by
conventional PCB and HDI. Unimicron has manufacturing sites in Taiwan and
China (Suzhou, Kunshan, Shenzhen). UMC is the largest shareholder, with a
~13% stake.

Investment strategy
We rate Unimicron shares a Buy. Our positive view is premised on: more
upside potential in the ABF segment thanks to product-mix improvements,
better pricing on more complex product upgrade, and capacity expansion.
Despite macro uncertainty, from a long-term perspective, we believe the
tightness in ABF is unlikely to ease without much new capacity ramped
aggressively in the upcoming years, and Unimicron should be the largest
beneficiary with its scale in ABF. Thanks to AI GPU/ASIC demand, we now see

strong growth in 2026/27E ABF consumption. We believe Unimicron could
see strong revenue growth again as it did in 2019–21 from both ABF new
capacity and a BT rebound.
Valuation
Our target price for Unimicron is NT$1,550. We use a 1-year forward-looking
DCF-based valuation (ie, end-2027) to reflect Unimicron's ability to generate
stable cash. With a risk-free rate of 5.0% (10-year government bond rate), a
market risk premium of 7%, and an equity beta of 1.43 (2-year weekly share

price), we calculate Unimicron's WACC as 14.6%. Our target price is equivalent
to 62x/30x 2026/27E EPS, which is higher than the +1std level of consensus
PE multiple of the past five years. However, as the market focuses on 2027
upcycle, we believe valuation on 2027E EPS is still in line with historical peak
valuations of 25–30x during the last upcycles.
Risks
Key downside risks that could mean Unimicron shares trade below our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected HDI business; 2) significant increase

in ABF supply in the industry; and 3) stronger-than-expected margin pressure
from depreciation due to higher capex investments.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 3

Delta Electronics (2308.TW)
29 October 2026 Citi Research
3
3Q Result Miss, Expecting Recovery in 4Q26
Delta’s 3Q earnings and GM were below expectation due to component shortages
and delays in data center deployment. GM declined 1.6ppts QoQ to 34.1%,
primarily reflecting inventory provisions equivalent to 1.7% of revenue, driven by
supply chain mismatches, customer delivery postponements, and product
specification adjustments. Nevertheless, OPM remained resilient at 16.6%, broadly

flat QoQ and YoY, supported by operating leverage.
Management indicated that AI-related businesses across its four divisions now
account for approximately 50% of consolidated revenue, highlighting Delta’s
increasing exposure to AI infrastructure investment. Management remains
confident in Delta’s competitive positioning, particularly its integrated capabilities
spanning power conversion, power distribution, thermal management, and
infrastructure solutions. Although the industry continues to face component

shortages, especially for certain power semiconductor devices, Delta believes its
supply chain management capabilities compare favorably to competitors. The
company also plans to establish a major manufacturing facility in Penang,
Malaysia, leveraging the region’s established infrastructure supply chain and
engineering talent.
Figure 1. Delta - 3Q26 earnings review
© 2026 Citigroup Inc. No redistribution without Citigroup’s written permission.
Source: Company Reports, Citi Research Estimates, Bloomberg consensus

HVDC Adoption and SOFC Commercialization Create
Additional Long- T erm Growth Opportunities
Delta expects the transition toward high-voltage DC power architecture to
accelerate as AI rack power density continues to increase. The company began
shipping ±400V HVDC solutions in 3Q26, while NVIDIA is promoting 800VDC
architectures for next-generation AI infrastructure. Management believes HVDC
adoption will eventually become widespread given its potential to improve power

conversion efficiency and reduce distribution losses, although standardization,
installation requirements, and shortages of qualified electrical workers could
constrain the near-term rollout. Beyond power conversion, Delta is developing
solid oxide fuel cell (SOFC) solutions to address growing demand for distributed
power generation. The company plans to begin commercial production in 2027,
with an initial manufacturing line providing approximately 100MW of annual

capacity, primarily supporting customer qualification and pilot installations. Initial

customers include domestic utility and industrial users, alongside several overseas
customers. Management emphasized that SOFC shipments will remain limited
initially as the company prioritizes product reliability and qualification.
4Q26 Recovery Expected as Inventory Normalizes, with
Positive Growth Outlook for 2027
Management expects revenue to increase sequentially in 4Q26 as delayed
customer deliveries resume and inventory begins to normalize. Gross margin

should improve from 3Q26 levels, although management remained cautious given
potential year-end inventory adjustments. The 3Q26 inventory provisions largely
reflected customer-specific products rather than structural obsolescence, and
some provisions could potentially be reversed following successful deliveries.
Nevertheless, management acknowledged that inventory buffers will likely remain
structurally higher than historical levels to accommodate volatile AI infrastructure

deployment schedules. Raw material costs have increased approximately 2% year-
to-date, prompting ongoing pricing negotiations with customers, with limited
recovery expected in 4Q26 and a more meaningful impact in 2027. Operating
expenses will continue increasing in absolute terms to support R&D and capacity
expansion. Management remains positive on 2027 revenue growth, although it did
not provide quantitative guidance. Separately, management expects no near-term

shipment disruption from the ongoing Vicor intellectual property litigation.
We lower earnings projection on power shortages, labor constraints, financing
conditions, and regulatory uncertainties could create quarterly shipment volatility,
but we believe its multiyear AI infrastructure growth cycle intact. We maintain Buy
with TP NT$1,715 (based on lower PER of 35x of 2027 EPS, from 40x previously)

### Page 7

Delta Electronics (2308.TW)
29 October 2026 Citi Research
7
Delta Electronics
Company description
Delta was established in 1975 and is the world's leading computer switching
power supply manufacturer. Its business is divided into: 1) Power Electronics
(59% of 2022 revenue), including Switching Power Supply, Components,
Display and EV Solution; 2) Infrastructure (27% of revenue), including Wind
power, Telecom power, UPS, EV Infrastructure, Networking; 3) Automation

(14% of revenue), including Industrial Automation (IA), Building Automation;
and 4) Others ( <1% of revenue).
Investment strategy
We rate Delta Electronics shares as Buy due to its strong execution and the
ability to expand margin through favorable product mix shift to AI power and
thermal solutions. We believe EV business slowdown is in the price, and we
expect margins to improve driven by AI solution spec upgrades and expanding
business scale. Delta is now positioning itself as an infrastructurer enabler

rather than a pure component supplier, which deepens its key position in the
AI supply chain.
Valuation
Our target price for Delta Electronics of NT$1,715 is based on 35x 2027E EPS
estimate. Our target multiple is set at the higher end of its trading range since
2023, which we believe is warranted by Delta’s strong earnings growth and
better product mix prospects. We also believe the company’s leading industry
position, long product development cycle, and strong engagement with

customers should translate into high orders/earnings visibility.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
are: 1) lower contribution from non-PC businesses; 2) products in the new
businesses (LED lighting, electric vehicle, and Voice) growing more slowly
than expected; and 3) PC power supply losing market share.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

## Document 146: 261030_daiwa_kinsus

Source PDF: all report pdf/261030_daiwa_kinsus.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: Kinsus reported 3Q26 results on 30 October. Net income came in at 
TWD2.39bn (EPS: TWD4.68), 8% below our estimate (TWD2.59bn) but 13% above the 
Bloomberg consensus (TWD2.12bn). Operating results were stronger than expected, 
driven mainly by an improving product mix, including a rising contribution from Nvidia CPU-
related products. While 3Q26 revenue of TWD14.54bn (up 16% QoQ and 40% YoY) was 
largely in line with expectations, gross margin reached 33.9%, 0.7ppt/0.9ppt above our 
estimate/the Bloomberg consensus. Operating margin reached 22.1%, versus our 20.5% 
estimate and the street’s 19.4%, on gross-margin expansion and higher operating 
leverage. However, weaker-than-expected non-operating profit and a higher minority 
interest (TWD373m) versus our estimate (TWD311m) resulted in the 8% net-profit miss 
(vs. our expectations).

Looking ahead, we expect 4Q26 revenue to increase 10% QoQ, driven mainly by ongoing 
price increases and product-mix optimisation, with further gross-margin expansion. We 
believe AMD’s new CPU platform (i.e., Venice) and BT substrates for optical transceivers 
could be the next growth drivers for Kinsus in 2027. Separately, Kinsus’s board approved 
an additional c.TWD60bn project budget for capacity expansion on 30 October, which we 
view as further evidence of tight supply conditions and improving demand visibility. We 
have a Buy (1) rating on Kinsus with a 12M TP of TWD1,580, based on 36x our 1-year-
forward EPS forecast. Key downside risks: weaker-than-expected demand for memory, 
PCs, gaming GPUs or AI servers.

What’s the impact: 
Below is our first take on Kinsus’s 3Q26 results, released on 30 October.

• 3Q26 operating results beat expectations; net profit missed our estimate. Net 
income came in at TWD2.39bn (EPS: TWD4.68), 8% below our estimate (TWD2.59bn) 
but 13% above the Bloomberg consensus (TWD2.12bn). Operating results were 
stronger than expected, driven mainly by an improving product mix, including a rising 
contribution from Nvidia CPU-related products. While 3Q26 revenue of TWD14.54bn (up 
16% QoQ and 40% YoY) was largely in line with expectations, gross margin reached 
33.9%, 0.7ppt/0.9ppt above our estimate/the Bloomberg consensus. Operating margin 
reached 22.1%, versus our 20.5% estimate and the street’s 19.4%, on gross-margin 
expansion and higher operating leverage. However, non-operating profit of TWD36m 
was 59%/72% below our/Bloomberg consensus forecasts, while higher-than-expected 
minority interest of TWD373m (vs. our TWD311m estimate) resulted in the 8% net-profit 
miss (vs. our expectations).

• Business outlook. Looking ahead, we expect 4Q26 revenue to increase 10% QoQ, 
driven mainly by ongoing price increases and product-mix optimisation. We expect 
gross margin to expand further on continued product-mix improvement. We believe 
AMD’s new CPU platform (i.e., Venice) and BT substrates for optical transceivers could 
be the next growth drivers for Kinsus in 2027. Separately, Kinsus’s board approved an 
additional c.TWD59.8bn project budget for capacity expansion on 30 October. This 
follows two project budgets of TWD23.5bn and TWD19.6bn announced in January and 
July 2026, respectively. The three projects would represent an aggregate approved 
budget of c.TWD102.9bn. We view the latest expansion plan as further evidence of tight 
supply conditions and stronger demand visibility.

What we recommend: 
We have a Buy (1) rating on Kinsus with a 12M TP of TWD1,580, based on a target PER 
of 36x applied to our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key downside risk: weaker-than-
expected demand for memory, PCs, gaming GPUs or AI servers.

Update
Hon Hai Precision  | Asia Pacific
Hon Hai Technology Day 2026
(HHTD 2026)
DSX POD accelerates AI infrastructure deployment: Unveiled at GTC Berlin and
displayed at HHTD26, NVIDIA’s DSX POD packages 7x VR200 NVL72 racks, 3x
Spectrum-6 SPX racks, up to ~25 exaFLOPS of NVFP4 performance, and over 10M
components per container ( Exhibit 1 ). Hon Hai said factory integration can shorten
deployment at prepared sites from six months to as little as 14 days, with initial

deliveries expected in early 2027.
Rack capacity and automation support Rubin ramp: HH has doubled AI rack
assembly capacity over the past year to ~2K racks/week. It said its cableless Vera
Rubin compute-tray design cuts assembly time from over two hours to ~20 minutes,
with a further target of five minutes. Greater automation should improve
throughput and consistency while reducing labor intensity, according to HH
management, supporting the Rubin ramp.

Three new EV models expand the CDMS portfolio: HH unveiled a MODEL A+, a
premium B-segment SUV; MODEL K+, an affordable lifestyle SUV for younger
buyers; and MODEL K, a compact truck targeting Japan’s kei segment and last-mile
logistics. The launches expand its EV reference portfolio to 12, broadening its
contract design and manufacturing service  offering across passenger and
commercial vehicles.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

研 究  報 告                
上品(4770.TT) 
產業：化學工業 
AI 需求帶動訂單攀升，擴產與高毛利產品推升獲利成⻑  
 投資評價與建議 
考量AI驅動全球半導體擴產 ， 帶動氟素設備 、 化學品槽⾞與建廠⼯程訂單成⻑ ，
且低毛利舊單消化、高毛利產品放量及兩岸產能擴充推升毛利率，參考同業日
本AGC、Daikin PE區間13-21倍，基於2027年EPS給予17倍PER。 
 重點評論分析 
AI需求推升訂單動能 ：3Q26單季營收季增22.5%至16.0億元 ， 受惠AI帶動國內
晶圓廠客戶持續建廠，推升氟素內襯設備與管道系統的需求，年初在手訂單38
億元陸續轉為實質營收貢獻，且低毛利訂單已消化完畢，預期在高毛利訂單出
貨下，推估毛利率進⼀步提升1.38ppts至37%，稅後EPS 4.46元，2026全年營
收年增18%至55.8億元，毛利率提升3.6ppts至36.58%，稅後EPS 14.45元。 
擴產與高階新品推升獲利成⻑ ：2027年受惠兩岸產能擴充及高單價槽⾞ 、 建廠
設備進入交貨高峰 ， 2026年槽⾞接單量已超越去年的2倍突破1000台 ， 台灣廠
月產能將由80台提升至140台，並規劃進⼀步擴至170台，中國嘉興廠亦將提
升產能20%，預計自1Q27起挹注營收，產品方面，高溫環境用離型膜、高純
度配件及過濾產品將隨客戶認證逐步放量 ， 帶動高毛利產品比重提升 ， PTFE低
介電材料則仍處配方測試階段，具中⻑期成⻑潛⼒，受惠在地化供應、⼯程整
合服務及產能利用率提升 ， 預估2027年營收年增25%至69.7億元 ， 毛利率提升
2.92ppts至39.5% ，費用率因非大幅建廠 ， 維持在11.8% ， 稅後EPS 19.84元 。  
 投資風險 
客戶擴產進度延遲、高階新品開發進度不如預期、原料成本壓抑獲利表現。

### Page 2

2 
| 公司簡介  
主要進口上游高階氟素樹脂 ， 如 PFA 、 PTFE 原料 ， 並自行加⼯為半成品的板材與管材 ， 以及製造終端高階等設備 ，
核心產品包括內襯設備(桶槽、反應釜)、內襯管道與管件、化學品運輸槽⾞以及⼯程服務等，這些產品主要應用於
先進半導體製程，包含 2nm 以下，與高純度電子級化學品，如硫酸、氫氟酸、光阻劑等的生產、儲存、輸送與廢
液回收系統，由於氟素材料具有極佳的耐酸鹼腐蝕與抗污染特性，設備能將金屬離子控制在 PPT 等級以下、顆粒
控制在 0.02 以下，為晶圓代⼯與特化廠維持極高良率與穩定品質的關鍵所在。 
截至 2Q26 產品別營收，內襯設備佔比 68.3%，為公司最核心的營收來源，包含特化廠與晶圓廠建廠所需的整套
重化線純化槽、儲罐、反應釜及槽⾞等設備，其他材料與半成品 16.7%，主要為第⼀階段加⼯的氟素板材與管材
半成品，多數留作自用，部分出口給海外(日本、歐洲)缺乏加⼯業者的獨立市場，內襯管道與管件 9.6%，用於連
結各機台設備與廠區內的化學品輸送管線系統，⼯程業務 5.4%，協助電子化學品廠內部的襯裡與管道施⼯，受惠
於市場技術人⼒缺乏，該業務毛利率較佳。 
公司在台灣彰濱設有主要生產基地與研發中心，並於中國浙江嘉興設有⼯廠及美國亞利桑那州設有子公司，截至
2Q26 地區別營收，台灣佔比 49.3%，為目前成⻑動能最強的區域，受惠於晶圓代⼯龍頭與特化廠強勁建廠需求，
台灣廠同時負責供應美洲及全球非中國市場、中國 44.8%，由中國廠獨立生產運作，專門供應中國內需半導體與
特化廠擴建需求、美國 3.4%，因當地晶圓廠建廠審核較慢且缺乏專業⼯程技術人⼒，推進時程較⻑，目前營收佔
比偏低、歐洲 1.5%、其他 1%。 
圖表、上品主要產品及產業概況

資料來源：玉山投顧、公司資料

| 營 運 概 況 
擺脫低谷舊單稀釋，產品組合高階化使毛利提升：1H26 合併營收 23.6 億元，YoY+4%，其中半導體產業佔營收
高達 91.3%，主要受惠全球晶圓代⼯龍頭與記憶體大廠積極擴產，現有特化廠產能無法滿足晶圓廠開出的需求，
帶動建廠與特化重化線設備強勁拉貨 ， 其餘為傳統石化產業占 6.4% ， 做為維持基礎⼯業需求的穩定貢獻 ， LCD 面
板與太陽能佔 1.2%，其他佔 1.1%，毛利率年減 1.7ppts 至 35.3%，下滑主因在於認列的營收中，相當大部分來
自 2025 年半導體與特化產業處於景氣低谷期時所接的舊訂單，因當時因市場需求薄弱、競爭較為激烈，產品平

### Page 3

3 
均毛利率較差且接單總量偏低，進而稀釋 1H26 整體毛利表現，其中 2Q26 單季毛利率季增 1.7ppts 至 35.6%，
顯示高毛利的訂單已逐漸回升，1H26 費用年增 25%至 3.25 億元，其中 2Q26 單季費用季增 4%至 1.65 億元，
主因包含公司為維持先進製程氟素設備的技術領先，研發支出佔營收比重較去年同期增加 1ppts，另外公司依據
會計準則提列了預期信用風險損失評估，約占費用的 2%，若扣除信用風險損失評估，實質營業費用率仍穩定維持
在 11-12%區間 ， 1H26 業外 0.44 億元 ， 主要來自利息收入增加 ， 以及美元與人⺠幣升值所帶來的匯兌收益 ， 2Q26
所得稅因集中認列未分配盈餘加徵 5%稅賦，有效稅率較 1Q26 拉升 7ppts 至 21.6%，稅後 EPS 5.63 元，3Q26
單季營收季增 22.5%至 16.0 億元，受惠於國內客戶持續建廠，年初在手訂單 38 億元轉為實質營收貢獻，且低毛
利訂單已消化完畢，推估毛利率進⼀步提升 1.38ppts 至 37.0%，稅後 EPS 4.46 元。

受惠全球 AI 市場需求與 ESG 回收政策，帶動氟素相關產品需求：截至八月底在手訂單已突破 75 億元，公司預估
2026 全年新接訂單將年增 91%，由於 AI 需求推升全球半導體晶圓廠展開大規模擴產，目前僅晶圓代⼯龍頭在台
灣與海外同時進行興建的晶圓廠就多達約 20 座，隨著客戶現場建廠與設備拉貨排程推進，主要電子級化學品相關
製造商均必須進行大規模擴產與建廠，另配合政府政策、ESG 環保法規，中鼎(9933)等大型⼯程統包商均跨入回
收領域，直接推升氟素內襯設備與管道系統的需求，化學品運輸槽⾞因應晶圓廠大量使用化學品，預估接單量將
突破 1,000 台，相較去年 600 台是翻倍成⻑，目前槽⾞設備的訂單交期已往後推三年陸續交貨，客戶嚴格把關進
程，確保原料與槽⾞交期不致拖累建廠進度，由於產出的大型槽體與設備體積龐大，產品製造完成入庫後，必須
待客戶建廠空間準備就緒並通知拉貨才能認列營收，因此單季成⻑趨勢比單月數據更為準確，預估 2026 全年營
收年增 18%至 55.8 億元，在成本與毛利率方面，上游氟素樹脂原料，如大金(Daikin)、科慕(Chemours)，全球出
現嚴重供不應求，交期⻑達 8 個月以上，供需缺口約在 5-10%，且面臨 PFAS 環保審查與附加稅費增加的壓⼒，
但公司為原料廠的⼀線直接採購客戶，享有優先供料保障，並具備從原始樹脂顆粒自製半成品(板材/管材)到終端
設備的能⼒，另外由於公司產品設備外殼高達 90%為白鐵(不鏽鋼)，透過高市佔率優勢，能將每⼀筆新接訂單動
態重新計價轉嫁給客戶來降低成本壓⼒，預期在高毛利的產品訂單陸續開出，使整體毛利率回升 3.6ppts 至
36.58%，稅前盈餘成⻑ 40%至 14.4 億元，稅後 EPS 14.45 元。

產能擴充，持續投入高階新產品開發：2027 年預期營收受惠高能見度槽⾞與建廠設備大批量交付認列，高單價
與高毛利設備將於明年進入出貨高峰，另外新擴充的產能部分，以槽⾞為單位，中國嘉興廠進行 20%的產能提
升、動線優化與組裝委外調整，台灣廠已從每月 80 台提升至 140 台，皆於 1Q27 開始貢獻，台灣廠預計明年規
劃再提升 20%至 170 台，有效紓解目前產能滿載的壓⼒，支撐明年營收大幅成⻑。 
公司持續投入新產品研發，其中離型膜應用於晶圓廠高溫區環境的關鍵新產品，已開始小量出貨，預計於 2026
年底完成送樣，明年貢獻營收，高純度配件與包材，包含關閉時僅留 90% 殘留的射出件接頭、100L ⼀體成型
槽⾞杯、新型取樣器、濾芯過濾產品等，隨著客戶認證通過，高毛利產品出貨比重將在明年進⼀步拉高，⼯程業
務高毛利貢獻持續挹注，主要因應特化廠建廠與回收系統需求，化學廠內部的管道與襯裡⼯程訂單持續增加，在
⼯程人⼒極度缺乏的市場環境下，公司內部⼯程團隊提供整合性服務，使⼯程業務維持高毛利率表現，持續挹注
明年獲利，由於同業競爭對手大多採取保守態度不敢大幅投資，公司在地化供應優勢與積極擴產與技術升級策
略，預估市佔率將持續攀升，有助於毛利率表現。 
另因應 AI 與高頻高速傳輸技術的突破，如 PTFE/氟素樹脂與銅箔結合的低介電常數與低介電損耗(Low Dk/Df)特
性需求迫切，公司與特定下游廠商合作，進行高黏性 PTFE 氟素基板材料與金屬銅箔結合的開發與測試，由於技
術非常高，氟素樹脂化學惰性極強，提升其高黏著性並與銅箔緊密貼合，同時保持極佳的高頻電性，屬於材料科
學上的高難度突破，目前開發進度尚未正式通過客戶驗證，仍處於配方調整測試階段，屬中⻑期高毛利產品線。

### Page 4

4 
預估 2027 全年成⻑ 25%至 69.7 億元，受惠兩岸新產能開出、高毛利槽⾞與高階新產品比重提升，毛利率提升
2.92ppts 至 39.5%，費用率因公司擴充產能主要仰賴人員技術培訓、廠區動線優化與前後段⼯序，非大幅興建
廠房，因此未來不會產生龐大折舊費用來墊高費用率，隨營業收入規模擴大而稀釋，預期維持在 11.8%，營益率
提升 3.6ppts 至 27.7%，稅前盈餘年成⻑ 40.8%至 20.2 億元，稅後 EPS 19.84 元。

1 
6239 力成(Powertech)

報告日期： 2026/10/28 研 究 員：翁浩軒  (Davy Wong)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 先進封裝比重提升，FOPLP 預期 2027 年放量貢獻 
 記憶體供需仍緊缺，稼動率維持高檔 
 訂單能見度高，預期明年營收獲利創高

一. 3Q26 毛利率優於預期，EPS 3.44 元 
力成 3Q26 營收 253.34 億(QoQ+9.6% 、YoY+26.87%) ，受惠於 DRAM
及 Logic 需求強勁，營收均季增約 15%，Nand 營收亦季增 5%。各
產品而言，DRAM 方面，訂單能見度高，推升營收穩定成長; Nand
方面，AI 伺服器帶旺 eSSD 需求，推升營收季增年增; Logic 業務
方面，出貨續增，營收季增年增雙位數; 超豐受到客戶需求強勁，
產品價格調整及簽訂 L T A 帶動營收季增年增雙位數;  T e r a  
Probe/TeraPower 營收持續季增年增雙位數。3Q26 毛利率受惠稼動
率提升而上升至 24.02%優於預期，唯營業費用較原先預期高，稅
後淨利 26.11 億(QoQ+17.71%、YoY+69.87%)，EPS 3.44 元。

二、4Q26 營收小幅季增，預估 EPS 4.18 元 
4Q26 預期記憶體、邏輯業務維持穩定成長，營收預估季增中低個
位數。DRAM 方面，記憶體產業仍處於供不應求加上客戶晶圓產
出極大化有利封測整體利用率，原材料成本上漲將適度反映至客
戶售價; NAND 方面，AI 帶動 SSD 及 Nand 封測需求維持高檔，且
隨封裝材料成本上升，力成將適度反映於封測價格上; Logic 業務
方面，受惠高階封裝 FCBGA 需求持續成長，整體產能利用率維持
高檔，FOPLP 客戶端產品驗證中，預期 2027 年量產。子公司 Tera 
Probe/Tera Power 受惠 HPC、AI、ADAS 及車用產品應用持續需求
的支撐，營收穩健成長，子公司超豐於 AI 與 HPC 帶動 Bumping
及 FC 封裝需求，菲律賓廠第四季起貢獻營收。預估力成 4Q26 營
收 262.21 億(QoQ+3.5%、YoY+22.49%)，毛利率 24.3%，稅後淨利
31.72 億(QoQ+21.46%、YoY+70.24%)，EPS 4.18 元。

三.  2026 / 2027 年獲利創高，預估 EPS 12.97 元/17.79 元 
展望 2026 年，AI 相關應用帶動公司 Memory 及 Logic 業務需求強
勁，預估今年逐季成長。Memory 業務方面，DRAM 及 Nand 封測
受惠於客戶拉貨積極，加上車用、網通、Server 等需求成長下，記
憶體位元出貨量穩定成長;隨著 AI 功能逐漸滲透到邊緣端及 AI 
投資評等：買進 
目標價位：320 元 
      18.0 X 2027 EPS 
參考價位：254.5 元 
     14.3X 2027 EPS 
     2026/10/27 收盤

PC 、AI 手機，預期將有助於推升 Memory 需求。Logic 業務方面，
公司在 Flip Chip 、Bumping 等高階封裝產能增加，且在客戶滲透率
慢慢提高，搭配子公司超豐、Tera Probe/Tera Power 需求升溫，營
收持續成長。預估力成 2026 年營收 959.85 億(YoY+28.1%)，毛利
率 22.54%，稅後淨利 98.47 億(YoY+77.881%)，EPS 12.97 元。展望
2027 年 ， 記憶體供不應求延續 ， 加上 AI 帶動 logic 晶片封裝需求，
營收獲利持續創高，公司積極投入高階封裝業務，基於其記憶體
封裝薄晶片與晶片堆疊之技術開發新型態封裝產品，公司投入之
FOPLP 可整合高階 IC 功能提高效能，降低成本，2027 年將明顯貢
獻營收。預估力成 2027 年營收 1166.97 億(YoY+21.58%)，毛利率
23.57%，稅後淨利 135.05 億(YoY+37.14%)，EPS 17.79 元。

四.投資建議：買進，目標價 320 元 
投資建議方面，力成近年逐步從傳統記憶體封測跨入邏輯業務，
並拓展高階封裝，成為全方位封測廠。公司受惠於記憶體封測及
高階封裝需求，今明年營運正向，考量到 1)公司在先進封裝與國
際大廠合作，FOPLP、光通訊將成為新成長動能、2)明年記憶體供
需仍緊缺，稼動率維持高檔、3)今明年營收獲利創高，參考 2027
年 EPS 給予 18 倍本益比，目標價 320 元，建議買進。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
力成科技設立於 1997 年 5 月，專注於記憶體之封裝測試業務，為
全球第五大封測廠。公司聚焦記憶體產品，在大股東全球最大記
憶體模組製造廠商金士頓的支持下，與國際大廠策略結盟，如美
光、東芝、Intel、Sandisk 等，而取得量大且穩定訂單。公司記憶
體封測同業包括日月光、矽品、南茂、華東、福懋等公司。公司
2011 入股超豐，邏輯 IC 封測佔營收提高到 20％以上(主攻通訊市
場之高階邏輯封測)。2025 年營收比重分別為：封裝占比 67%，測
試占比 22%、SIP/Module 占 11%。從產品類別看，動態隨機存取記
憶體(DRAM)占比 22%，快閃記憶體(Flash)占比 25%，邏輯 IC 占比
42%、SIP/Module 占比 11%。

二.產業趨勢概況  
1. 力成持續擴充先進封裝佈局，FOPLP 將於 2027 放量 
力成為全球第一大記憶體封測大廠 ， 客戶為全球 DRAM 、 Nand Flash
大廠，近年公司積極拓展 logic 業務，營收比重已占四成以上，預
估未來邏輯業務將穩定貢獻營收，公司積極推進高階封裝佈局，
提供 2.5D、3D IC 封裝產品，其中 FOPLP 已取得客戶認證，公司
將以 FOPLP 提供 AMD AI 晶片，隨著機台陸續到位，預估 2027 年
上半年開始量產，因應客戶需求及訂單能見度提升，新購入 P12
廠區(購自友達)無塵室已建置完畢，將做 on substrate 的部分 ，而因
應 FOPLP 產品測試需求，子公司 TeraPower 亦新購置廠房，用於

公司研究報告

4 
CP 、FT 測試，預估 2026~2027 年將建 400~500 台測試機台。另外，
公司亦與 Broadcom 在新加坡成立合資公司 ， 以力成在 FOPLP 封裝
經驗基礎下，共同開發及生產先進封裝基板上之加成式細線寬之
重佈層封裝技術，另外 Broadcom 亦帶領力成進入光通訊領域，預
期今年底小量生產 microbump Based OE 、 2027 下半年開始生產 CPO 
Swtich、2028 年量產 CPO AI 晶片。未來力成將跳脫傳統記憶體封
測廠，提供全方位封測服務。

2. AI 帶動 Logic 及 Memory 業務需求強勁 
AI 及雲端基礎建設投資強勁，持續支撐半導體及記憶體產業需
求，目前 DRAM 與 NAND 需求持續大於供給，產品價格趨勢有利
於記憶體產業營收成長 ， AI 相關應用將持續帶動公司成長 。 DRAM
方面，AI 強勁需求使整體記憶體仍然供不應求，整體產能利用率
可望維持高檔水準，並待客戶新晶圓產能陸續開出。隨著市場供
需持續緊俏，客戶對原物料成本上漲帶動的價格調整亦展現高接
受度。 Nand &SSD 方面， AI 應用推動企業級 SSD 及 NAND 相關需
求維持強勁，而封裝材料成本持續上升，力成將適度反映於客戶
價格，預期對營收及毛利率具正面效益。

Logic 業務方面， AI 市場持續增溫，力成在高階 FCBGA 同步受惠，
既有高階產品需求亦持續擴大，而先進封裝 FOPLP 方面，客戶端
產品驗證持續推進，預期 2027 年量產，此外隨著市場需求及量產
信心度提升，客戶在新產品導入 (NPI) 的需求亦顯著增加，力成除
持續推進 5 倍 reticle 的封裝應用外 ， 更進一步投入超大尺寸封裝技
術的研發，以因應未來 AI 晶片對高效能、高整合度的大尺寸封裝
需求。在高階 FCBGA 領域，第三季成功導入大晶片 (1x reticle Size)
封裝 (75x78) 的量產後，第四季將進一步跨入更大尺寸封裝領域
(78x88) ，整合 SoC Die 與 DRAM 元件在單一基板上。

## Document 203: 9月规模以上工业利润数据解读：9月规模以上工业利润总额同比明显提高

Source PDF: all report pdf/9月规模以上工业利润数据解读：9月规模以上工业利润总额同比明显提高.pdf
Report source: 国新证券股份
Date: 2026-11-01
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

市场研究部 2026年10月27日
事件
10 月 27 日，国家统计局发布，1-9 月，全国规模以上工业企
业实现利润总额 60337.4 亿元，同比增长 15.5%（按可比口径计
算）。
解读
一、1-9 月规上工业利润同比保持较高增长，9 月增速比 8 月
明显提高
1-9月，全国规模以上工业企业实现利润总额 60337.4 亿元，
同比增长 15.5%，比 1-8 月回落 0.2 个百分点，比 2025 年同期提
高 12.3个百分点，创 2022年以来同期同比增速新高。
其中，9月，规模以上工业企业利润总额同比增长 14.4%，比
8月提高 10.2个百分点，比 2025年 9月回落 7.2个百分点。
1-9 月，采矿业利润总额同比增长 38.2%，比 1-8 月提高 3.1
个百分点，制造业利润总额同比增长 17.0%，比 1-8月回落 0.4个
百分点，电力热力水的生产供应业同比-14.3%，降幅比 1-8月扩大
2.3个百分点。
9月，规模以上工业利润总额同比增速明显提高，主要受 PPI

上涨的影响。9月 PPI环比上涨 1.2%，比 8月提高 0.8个百分点，
同比上涨 5.0%，涨幅比 8 月提高 1.2 个百分点，环比同比涨幅均
扩大，当月利润改善明显。
二、1-9 月股份制企业利润较高增长，行业利润增速极端分化
1-9月，国有控股规模以上工业企业利润总额同比增长 13.1%，
比 1-8月提高 2.8个百分点；股份制企业利润总额同比增长 20.8%，
比 1-8月提高 0.4百分点；外商及港澳台商投资企业利润总额同比
增长 0.5%，比 1-8 月回落 1.8 个百分点；私营企业利润总额增长
8.7%，比 1-8月回落 1.7个百分点。
1-9 月，在 39 个可比工业大类行业中，17 个行业利润同比增
长，22个行业利润总额同比下降。
在利润总额超千亿的行业中，计算机、通信和其他电子设备制
造业利润同比增长 103.8%，有色金属冶炼和压延加工业利润同比
增长 79.7%，有色金属矿采选业利润总额同比增长 68.0%，化学原
宏观研究
9月规模以上工业利润总额同比明显提高

——9月规模以上工业利润数据解读
市场表现截至 2026.9.30
资料来源：国家统计局,国新证券整理。
分析师：郝大明
登记编码：S1490514010002
邮箱：haodaming@crsec.com.cn
证券研究报告

### Page 2

料和化学制品制造业利润同比增长 49.2%，煤炭开采和洗选业利润
同比增长 57.0%，石油和天然气开采业利润同比增长 21.3%。
1-9月，汽车制造业、酒饮料和精制茶制造业、电气机械和器
材制造业、电力热力生产和供应业利润总额同比分别-22.8%、-
14.3%、-5.9%、-17.9%。
三、重点产业链利润增速较高
电子相关行业利润高速增长。1-9 月，电子行业利润同比增长
1.0倍，比 1-8月回落 10个百分点。其中，电子专用材料制造、半
导体分立器件制造行业利润分别增长 2.2倍、51.7%；新一代信息
技术加速发展，带动光电子器件制造、电子电路制造行业利润分别
增长 69.8%、62.5%。
高技术制造业引领作用明显。1-9 月，规模以上高技术制造业
利润同比增长 52.0%，比 1-8 月回落 2.7个百分点。其中，光纤制
造、光缆制造行业利润分别增长 5.7倍和 1.1 倍；计算机外围设备
制造、计算机整机制造、工业控制计算机及系统制造行业利润分别
增长 2.8倍、1.7倍、90.9%。

原材料制造业利润快速增长。1-9 月，规模以上原材料制造业
利润同比增长 44.9%，比 1-8 月回落 2.4个百分点。其中，化学原
料和化学制品制造业利润增长 49.2%，比 1-8月回落 1.8个百分点；
有色金属冶炼和压延加工业利润同比增长 79.7%，比 1-8 月回落
3.2 个百分点；石油加工行业同比扭亏为盈，实现利润总额 842.2
亿元，比 1-8月增加 279.3亿元。
总体看，1-9月原材料制造业、高技术制造业、电子行业等行
业利润大幅增长，规模以上工业企业利润较高增长。9月 PPI环比、
同比涨幅扩大，工业利润增速明显提高。预测四季度工业利润同比
增速将比三季度小幅提高。
投资建议
1：1-9月规上原材料制造业利润总额同比较高增长。
2：1-9月规上新高技术制造业利润高增长。
3：1-9月规上电子相关行业利润高增长。
风险提示
1、地缘政治恶化超预期；2、欧美经济衰退超预期。

## Document 281: Anthropic IPO前更新招股书凸显盈利压力：Q3营收近170亿美元、同比增11倍，净亏损扩大92%，算力承诺近9000亿

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic IPO前更新招股书凸显盈利压力：Q3营收近170亿美元、同比增11倍，净亏损扩大92%，算力承诺近9000亿.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

新招股书显⽰，今年前三季度， Anthropic 营收超 330 亿美元、同⽐增 12 倍；三季度运营费
⽤逾 175 亿美元、超过营收，同⽐增 2.9 倍，其中算⼒及基础设施⽀出增 3.8 倍⾄ 98.6 亿，同
⽐增速远超⼆季度的 48% ；三季度经营业绩由⼆季度盈利 9.7 亿美元转亏 5.67 亿；到三季度
末承诺⽀出 8950 亿美元，其中 4475 亿在 2030 年底前发⽣，将近 700 亿需此后⼀年内⽀付。
摘要：
Anthropic IPO 前更新的招股书显⽰，尽管 Claude ⺟公司营收继续⾼速增⻓，但算⼒成本和⻓期
基础设施投⼊也在急剧膨胀，第三季度经营业绩由盈转亏、净亏损同⽐扩⼤，近 9000 亿美元的
算⼒及基础设施承诺⽀出进⼀步凸显其盈利压⼒。
根据 Anthropic 周⼀向美国证券交易委员会（ SEC ）提交的修订版 S-1 招股书，公司第三季度营收
达到 169.56 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 1127% ；净亏损达到 232.91 亿美元，较上年同期的 121.38 亿美元

扩⼤约 92% 。截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇及此后，公司已签订的计算能⼒采购及租赁协议承诺⽀出总额约为
8954 亿美元。
企业客⼾持续扩张，前三季度营收超 330 亿美元
Anthropic 第三季度营收达到 169.56 亿美元，较第⼆季度的 115.55 亿美元环⽐增⻓约 46.7% ，较
2025 年同期的 13.82 亿美元增⻓ 1127% 。这意味着公司单季营收已达到⼀年前的约 12.3 倍。
从前三季度整体表现来看， Anthropic 今年前九个⽉累计营收达到 332.42 亿美元，远⾼于去年同
期的 25.45 亿美元，增幅约为 12 倍。企业客⼾的持续扩张是推动收⼊增⻓的重要动⼒。
截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇， Anthropic 过去 12 个⽉贡献⾄少 10 万美元收⼊的企业客⼾超过 1.19 万家，其中，
过去 12 个⽉贡献⾄少 100 万美元收⼊的客⼾超过 1900 家，贡献⾄少 1000 万美元收⼊的客⼾约
250 家。
不过，营收⾼速增⻓的同时， Anthropic 的净亏损也在扩⼤。第三季度净亏损为 232.91 亿美元，

同⽐扩⼤约 92% ，较第⼆季度净亏损额 663.09 亿美元环⽐收窄空昂孔明 65% ，但主要受到可转
换票据、衍⽣负债及 SAFE 协议公允价值变动等⾮现⾦项⽬影响，不能简单理解为公司核⼼经营
亏损同步收窄。
算⼒成本激增，经营业绩较⼆季度由盈转亏
营收增⻓的另⼀⾯，是 Anthropic 为训练和运⾏ AI 模型付出的成本迅速攀升。
修订版招股书显⽰， Anthropic 第三季度总运营费⽤达到 175.23 亿美元，较上年同期的 44.71 亿美
元增⻓约 292% ，已经超过当季 169.56 亿美元的营收。
其中，算⼒及基础设施⽀出达到 98.63 亿美元，较上年同期的 20.47 亿美元增⻓约 382% ，相当于
当季营收的约 58% 。相⽐之下，这⼀⽐例在第⼆季度约为 48% ，显⽰算⼒投⼊加速正在给利润
率带来压⼒。
受成本上升影响， Anthropic 第三季度经营亏损达到 5.67 亿美元，⽽第⼆季度公司曾实现 9.74 亿
美元经营利润。这也是公司在连续多个季度经营亏损后，于第⼆季度⾸次实现经营盈利，随后

⼜在第三季度重新陷⼊经营亏损。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

Anthropic 解释称，第三季度经营业绩由盈转亏，主要受到两⽅⾯因素影响：⼀是约 50 亿美元通
过短期承诺采购的算⼒成本，这类算⼒⽬前明显贵于多年期合同采购的同类算⼒；⼆是约 20 亿
美元与员⼯捐赠匹配计划有关的⾮现⾦费⽤。
不过，剔除股权激励、⾮现⾦慈善捐赠等项⽬后， Anthropic 第三季度调整后经营利润仍达到
24.63 亿美元，⾼于第⼆季度的 22.35 亿美元。但调整后经营利润率从第⼆季度的 19% 降⾄第三
季度的 15% ，反映出更昂贵的短期算⼒采购仍在侵蚀公司的利润空间。
这也意味着， Anthropic 虽然已经证明了 AI 产品具备强劲的商业化能⼒，但能否将营收增⻓持续
转化为经营利润，仍取决于算⼒成本下降的速度能否跟上业务扩张。
近 9000 亿美元算⼒承诺，近半将在 2030 年底前兑现
⽐单季度成本更引⼈关注的，是 Anthropic 已经锁定的⻓期算⼒和基础设施⽀出。
修订版招股书披露，截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇及此后，公司签订的算⼒采购承诺和租赁协议总额约为 8954 亿

美元，其中绝⼤部分与计算能⼒及基础设施有关。
从⽀出时间表来看，约 4475 亿美元预计将在 2030 年 12 ⽉ 31 ⽇前兑现，近半数承诺⽀出将在未来
四年多内陆续落地；另有约 698 亿美元将在截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇后的 12 个⽉内、即 2027 年 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇之前
⽀付。其余绝⼤部分承诺预计将在 2031 年⾄ 2045 年间兑现。
Anthropic 表⽰，随着计算需求⼤幅增⻓，公司正在从单纯依赖固定期限云计算协议，转向结合
直接租赁芯⽚、专⽤数据中⼼等⽅式的混合模式，希望通过建设⾃有专⽤基础设施，逐步以成
本更低的⻓期算⼒替代⾼价短期采购。
不过，这些承诺也意味着巨⼤的资⾦压⼒。公司在招股书中警告，部分算⼒采购协议包含最低
采购承诺和照付不议条款，即使实际使⽤量不⾜，公司仍可能需要⽀付相关费⽤。
更值得注意的是， Anthropic 的收⼊主要依赖客⼾实际使⽤量，并⾮全部来⾃⻓期合同承诺；⽽
算⼒和基础设施⽀出却往往需要提前多年锁定。如果客⼾需求增⻓不及预期，或者公司⽆法及

时将新增算⼒转化为收⼊，可能⾯临现⾦流压⼒，甚⾄难以履⾏相关合同义务。
截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇， Anthropic 拥有约 1466 亿美元流动性，包括 956 亿美元现⾦及现⾦等价物、 363 亿
美元短期投资，以及 148 亿美元信贷额度可⽤资⾦。公司表⽰，未来将通过业务收⼊和融资筹集
资⾦⽀持相关投资。
IPO 估值⾯临重新审视， AI 商业模式接受考验
Anthropic 此次更新招股书，正值其筹备潜在的重磅 IPO 之际。
公司已于 10 ⽉ 26 ⽇⾸次公开提交招股书，计划在纳斯达克全球精选市场上市，股票代码为
ANT 。本次修订⽂件补充了第三季度财务数据，也进⼀步披露了公司的算⼒采购和⻓期⽀出安
排。
据此前报道， Anthropic 有望在美国感恩节假期前完成 IPO ，融资规模可能⽐肩甚⾄超过 SpaceX
今年 6 约创纪录的 862 亿美元 IPO 融资。⾼盛和摩根⼠丹利担任此次 IPO 的牵头承销商，另有 20 家
银⾏参与。
然⽽，随着最新财务数据披露，投资者对 Anthropic 估值的判断也开始出现变化。

## Document 291: Belgium’s budget crisis is serious, but not hopeless

Source PDF: all report pdf/Belgium’s budget crisis is serious, but not hopeless.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Belgium's budget talks have hit a wall, but the crisis is not yet
at a dead end. A deal on €10 billion in savings remains our base
case, though political red lines need to soften. A watered-down
compromise would leave debt risks unresolved, while a
government collapse could put investor conﬁdence under
pressure
Philippe Ledent
Belgian Prime Minister Bart De Wever tendered his resignation to the King on
Monday
For several weeks, parties in the Belgian federal government have

been trying to agree on the main budget measures for the next
three years. The aim is to reach an agreement delivering €10 billion
in recurring savings in the federal budget (which also includes social
security). This would not only oﬀset the automatic increase in
expenditure linked to population ageing and interest payments on
the debt, but also reduce the deﬁcit from around 5% of GDP this
year to close to 4% of GDP in 2029.

### Page 2

However, last weekend, Prime Minister Bart De Wever had to
acknowledge that the negotiations had failed on two fronts. First,
given the time needed for parliament to debate and vote on the
legislation, it will no longer be possible to start 2027 with a duly
approved budget. Second, there are too many sticking points for a
political agreement to be reached at this stage.
What are the sticking points?
Speciﬁcally, the negotiations have been complicated by the

withdrawal of a key proposal from the Prime Minister. He had
proposed an across-the-board increase in the VAT rate, which would
have raised around €4 billion. Although several parties were
reluctant, one ruled it out altogether, forcing De Wever to take the
proposal oﬀ the negotiating table. Since then, however, other
parties have also set red lines (on social security savings, for
example) or insisted on measures that must be included (notably
broader taxation of ﬁnancial wealth). Finding a compromise has

therefore become more diﬃcult.
Furthermore, the social tensions aﬀecting many European
countries make it harder to agree on austerity measures. Any
spending cut is diﬃcult to implement, but a degree of social
cohesion must be maintained to avoid recreating in Belgium the
severe tensions seen in France. The challenge is therefore to strike
the right balance, which is harder to achieve than it would be
during a period of strong economic growth.
The government remains in ofﬁce for now

Faced with the parties’ conﬂicting red lines and diﬃculties in the
negotiations, De Wever drew the consequences and tendered his
resignation to the King on Monday. To the outside world, this could
seem like the fall of the government – but some perspective is
needed.
First, King Philippe has not yet accepted the Prime Minister’s
resignation. While he does not exercise personal political authority,
he can play an important consultative role at moments of political

uncertainty. He has deferred his decision until Friday. In the

### Page 3

meantime, the King will meet the leaders of the federal coalition
parties to hear their views and call for a compromise.
Second, by oﬀering his resignation, the Prime Minister probably
wants to send a clear signal to the other parties, warning them that
the price of failed negotiations would be very high. Indeed, the
latest opinion polls show that, if new elections were held, the
parties forming the current majority would not be able to renew it.

The far right would very likely gain ground in the Flemish part of the
country, as would the far left in the French-speaking part. This is
also a signal to voters across the coalition parties. Their party will
have to accept measures that run counter to its programme, but
that is the price to pay to put public ﬁnances on a sound footing,
keep the current coalition together and avoid a political crisis.
Third, the King begins his consultations today, but there have been

numerous political reactions since the weekend. Within the
majority, all have conﬁrmed a willingness to continue negotiations
and called for a sense of responsibility. Nobody wants the
government to fall. In the opposition, one party has even oﬀered its
support to form an alternative majority, which also puts pressure
on some coalition parties to accept compromises within the current
majority.
What is the likely scenario?
It is diﬃcult to predict what will happen. Tensions are high, and

although nobody wants the government to fall, this cannot be
ruled out. Our base case nevertheless remains that, following this
week’s consultations and discussions between the parties, more
time will be granted to reach an agreement on the €10bn in savings
over the next three years. After this week’s political shock, an
agreement would ultimately be reached. It would be too late to
start 2027 with a budget approved by parliament, but the damage
would be relatively limited. A mechanism exists to temporarily

“extend” the 2026 budget until the 2027 budget is duly approved.
If the parties fail to reach an agreement, there are two alternative
scenarios:

1. The parties manage to reach a minimal agreement on a
budget for 2027, involving a smaller ﬁscal eﬀort of around €3-4

billion (the Prime Minister had already proposed this option),
abandoning a multi-year planned eﬀort. However, even if the
government commits to continuing discussions for subsequent
years, it will be diﬃcult to go much further. The government
would then remain in oﬃce, but the eﬀort needed to put public
ﬁnances on a sound footing and make the debt trajectory
sustainable would not be delivered in the short term. Belgium
would gain some breathing space but would remain exposed

to the risk of weaker investor conﬁdence.
2. The deadlock is too severe, leaving the King with no choice but
to accept the government’s resignation. A period of
considerable uncertainty would then begin. Further initiatives
might still be launched to form a new majority, but new
elections would be more likely. The current government would
remain in oﬃce in a caretaker capacity, and the 2026 budget
would be extended into 2027. Without savings measures, the

public deﬁcit would continue to rise, and the election results
could make forming a new coalition very diﬃcult. This scenario
would clearly leave Belgium more exposed to investor distrust.
The situation would closely resemble that currently being faced
in France. To be clear, this scenario cannot be entirely ruled out,
but its probability remains low for now.

## Document 317: COP31_ Why it still matters for businesses

Source PDF: all report pdf/COP31_ Why it still matters for businesses.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Is COP still worth following? We think so. What matters for
businesses is whether this year’s COP31 can improve the
business case for action. Here are four areas to watch

Coco Zhang and Gerben Hieminga
The global climate landscape has changed signiﬁcantly since world
leaders committed 11 years ago to limiting global warming to well
below 2°C and pursuing eﬀorts to limit warming to 1.5°C.
Geopolitical tensions, policy shifts, trade protectionism, rising
energy demand… they have all made it harder for governments to
turn climate commitments into action.
That raises an important question for businesses: Is the Conference
of the Parties (COP) still worth following?
Our answer is yes.
Businesses do not just invest in climate targets; they invest in viable
projects and operations. But many of them still face economic and
practical barriers. Grid constraints and high electricity costs can
slow electriﬁcation. Greener industrial products can struggle to
Cookie settings

preferences here at any time.

Essential

Conﬁrm selection Accept all

### Page 2

attract buyers willing to pay a premium. And climate adaptation
plans do not always translate into investable projects.
Businesses cannot solve these challenges alone. In a 2025 UN
survey of more than 2,000 CEOs across 128 countries, 92% said
strong global governance and coordinated policy are “critical” or
“important” to implementing their sustainability agendas.
That is why COP is still relevant. It can help align policy priorities,
improving investment conditions, and connecting businesses with

the right resources. At COP31, four areas will be particularly
important for businesses to watch.
Electriﬁcation marries economic growth
with sustainability
Electriﬁcation is increasingly important for economic growth,
energy security, and sustainability. Demand from AI, EVs, heat
pumps and industrial decarbonisation is accelerating electricity
use, which is growing twice as fast as overall energy demand.
Electricity also punches above its weight economically, accounting

for 23% of global ﬁnal energy consumption but powering 46% of
global GDP. This implies that high value-added sectors rely more on
electricity than the overall economy.
Electricity’s economic contribution
outweighs its share of energy use

### Page 3

Source: ING Research, International Energy Agency
COP31’s push to accelerate electriﬁcation reﬂects this trend. The
COP31 presidency has set a goal of increasing electricity’s share of
ﬁnal energy consumption from 23% today to 35% by 2035.
Getting there will require addressing a major constraint: the grid. As
we have written, grid congestion could slow electriﬁcation, increase
costs, and delay electricity-dependent economic activity.
The key question at COP31 is how governments turn this ambition

into action. We think eﬀective action could include:
Establish a detailed electriﬁcation roadmap with interim
benchmarks to guide progress and sustain momentum across
future COPs
Enhance knowledge-sharing platforms. The International
Smart Grid Action Network has extensive experience sharing
best practices, but could go further by translating knowledge
into direct technical assistance and ready-to-deploy models
Set country- or sector-level targets and pathways to encourage

industry alignment and commitment
Improve access to ﬁnance by expanding dedicated ﬁnancing
for developing countries, while lowering upfront costs in Europe
Improve the economics. Coordinate energy pricing reforms to
make electriﬁcation more competitive with fossil fuel
combustion alternatives, including by reducing taxes and grid
fees that raise electricity prices
Strengthen infrastructure by encouraging governments to
reduce permitting barriers (e.g. Europe and the US), advance

### Page 4

regulatory support for power demand ﬂexibility, and expand
cross-border grids where needed (e.g. Southeast Asia)
If COP31 delivers meaningful action in these areas, the implications
for businesses could be signiﬁcant. Stronger policy could accelerate
near-term grid connections and enhance long-term investment
conﬁdence. Meanwhile, companies may need to work more closely
with utilities and regulators to secure the power they need.
Green industrialisation remains critical

But electriﬁcation is only part of the story. Industry is another
major source of process emissions, with sectors such as steel,
cement, and aviation accounting for roughly 20% of the global
total. Cutting these emissions is particularly diﬃcult because many
industrial processes still have few cost-eﬀective alternatives without
lengthy site overhauls.
COP31 has therefore put green industrialisation high on the
agenda, building on COP30’s progress. Following the Belém

Declaration on Global Green Industrialisation at COP30, a coalition
led by the Brazilian and UK governments mobilised hundreds of
millions of dollars in concessional ﬁnance for Brazilian industries
and awarded funding to 12 hydrogen projects. Over time, these
eﬀorts could reshape manufacturing processes and investment.
But many industrial decarbonisation technologies still face
signiﬁcant barriers to scaling. Their high cost premiums mean
economics depend heavily on government support. Demand for

cleaner industrial products remains limited. And the necessary
infrastructure for many is still underdeveloped.
Hard-to-abate sectors’ green premium is
way higher than what companies are willing
to pay

### Page 5

Source: World Economic Forum, ING Research
COP31 is not setting a new level of ambition for green
industrialisation. It’s focusing on implementing the existing
agenda. One priority is narrowing the demand gap. COP31 can
coordinate governments to create more demand for low-carbon
industrial products through long-term public procurement. If such
eﬀorts gain momentum at COP31, they could provide the long-run
revenue certainty investors need to back green industrial projects at

scale.
Climate ﬁnance faces multiple gaps
To realise the targets and priorities set forth at COP, investment is
crucial. And total ﬂows are growing. Global climate ﬁnance reached
around $2tn in 2024 and continued to grow in 2025, although at a
slower rate.
Global climate ﬁnance continues to grow

But the headline number masks signiﬁcant gaps. First, even though
climate mitigation (i.e. emissions reduction) ﬁnance accounted for
$1.9tn of the $2tn total, the level falls well short of estimated
needs, which range from $7.6tn-$9.6tn through 2050. In areas such
as electriﬁcation and green industrialisation, funding is needed not
only for technologies, but also for the infrastructure and demand

needed to scale them.
Second, only $64bn of the total went exclusively to adaptation, an
area requiring surging investment because of intensifying climate
risks. Third, a substantial share of climate ﬁnance stays within
advanced economies or comes from domestic sources.
International ﬂows to help developing countries totalled only
$175bn, far below COP29’s target of $1.3tn per year by 2035.

Source: ING Research, Climate Policy Initiative, UN, UNEP, Natural
Resources Defense Council
More climate products and solutions are emerging in the latter two
segments. But their higher risks or weaker business cases can make
it diﬃcult to attract capital without supportive policies. Investors are
actively turning to blended ﬁnance, which uses public capital to
help de-risk projects and mobilise private investment. Yet many still
lack the data, resources and connections needed to move projects

forward. This is where COP31 can help. As we discuss in the next
section, it can help governments create more favourable
investment conditions and connect suppliers, buyers and investors.
Adaptation is becoming a business
imperative and opportunity
Business awareness of climate adaptation has grown, as many
businesses are experiencing more disruptions ﬁrsthand. Our
previous analysis also shows how drought on Europe’s Rhine River,
hurricanes in the US, and other extreme weather events are already

aﬀecting supply chains across sectors. In aggregate, physical
climate risks could cost companies in the S&P Global 1200 index
$1.2tn annually by 2050.
One barrier to corporate investment in climate adaptation is that it
is still seen primarily as a way to avoid losses. But adaptation can
also create business value. Investments in resilient assets, cooling
systems, supplier diversiﬁcation and climate-resilient materials can
improve reliability, competitiveness and long-term growth.

Research from the World Resources Institute suggests that every $1
invested in adaptation can generate up to $10 in beneﬁts. That
return could be even higher for highly climate-exposed sectors. For
Cookie settings
At ING we respect your right to choose how your data is used.

### Page 8

example, the World Economic Forum estimates returns of $19–$43
per dollar invested in the food and beverage sector.
COP can help turn adaptation priorities into investable projects.
Since the Paris Agreement, countries have developed National
Adaptation Plans (NAPs), but many remain high-level and lack
speciﬁc, actionable projects. Recent COP eﬀorts have sought to
change this by encouraging governments to translate their NAPs
into portfolios of investable adaptation projects.

But identifying projects does not guarantee ﬁnancing. This is where
COP31 could make a diﬀerence. Its proposed Climate
Implementation Bridge (BRIDGE) initiative could make projects
easier for investors to identify and assess by centralising
information on technical requirements, risks, costs and beneﬁts.
Expanding the initiative to more countries could give more banks,
investors and companies access to potential adaptation
investments.
For businesses, a successful COP31 could therefore help unlock

capital for adaptation projects. The beneﬁts could extend
throughout supply chains, as companies increasingly depend on
the climate resilience of their suppliers to maintain reliable
operations.
Conclusion
The most eﬀective corporate sustainability strategies support long-
term growth, but they are more eﬀective with international
coordination and strong policy support. COP can bring
governments and businesses together to provide such a
framework, even amid geopolitical tensions and concerns over

slowing progress. COP31 may not deliver a landmark global
agreement, but practical advances in these four priorities could still
have immediate relevance for businesses.

Downgrade of global 
equities to a Core allocation  
Summary 
Event: We are downgrading global equities from Overweight 
to a Neutral/Core allocation. We continue to maintain a 
Neutral allocation to fixed income, Overweight on Gold and 
Underweight on Cash. There are more details in our Global 
Market Outlook publication on 30 October 2026. 
What does the equity downgrade mean?  
This downgrade implies a slightly lower allocation to global 
equities within a diversified portfolio. For our CIO Foundation 
Balanced portfolio, this would imply an equity allocation of 
52.5%, down from 53 .2%. We continue to expect positive 
returns for global equities  in the coming 6 -12 months , 
though equity investors may need to recalibrate expectations 
lower, compared with the 15 -20% annualised gains 
experienced in the last few years. Our capital market 
assumptions still suggest a mid -to-high single digit annual 
returns over the next few years, consistent with a transition 
to a mid -cycle bull market . Consensus estimates point to 
16% earnings growth for global equities in 2027, which 
should overcome various headwinds to deliver positive 
returns for equities over the next 12 months. Investors should 
thus, continue to maintain exposure to equities  within a 
diversified portfolio. 
In addition, we see a changing playbook  to prepare for 
higher volatility, more active management and greater 
diversification, as discussed below. 
What does it mean for regional equities 
positioning?

and Asia ex -Japan (AxJ) equities , which are expected to 
deliver the highest earnings growth in 2027 amongst the 
major regions. This growth is led by the technology sector, 
which carries the largest weight in both regions. Within the 
diversified equities portfolio, we maintain Core allocations to

structured to return principal or partial principal at maturity.

What are the implications on fixed income 
and FX markets? 
Bonds: This year’s surge in bond yields means bonds now 
offer a hedge against any equity market weakness. We 
believe the 3 -7-year part of the US bond yield curve offers 
the best hedge against any equity sell -off caused by AI -
related growth worries. This part of the bond curve has priced 
in most Fed rate hike expectations, which could reverse if 
growth concerns return. Meanwhile, we favour US Treasury

fuel price-led inflation scare.  
Credit: For corporate bonds, adopting a defensive stance in 
a volatile environment typically involves: i) increasing credit 
quality, favouring higher-rated issuers; and ii) moving up the 
capital structure, by reducing exposure to subordinated debt 
and favouring secured debt. From a sector perspective, 
large, diversified banks, utilities, and quasi -sovereigns tend 
to be more defensive, particularly relative to cyclical sectors, 
such as metals and mining, exploration, and technology.         
FX: We favour bearish AUD/JPY as a tactical hedge against 
any equity market correction. Review of 21 past S&P500 
correction sessions (2010 -2025) shows AUD weakened 
against USD in 86% of cases, while JPY strengthened 
against USD in 67%, supporting our bearish AUD/JPY 
outlook during any equity market stress.

## Document 398: Quantum dictionary, part 2_ From potential to progress We go beyond the quantum basics to explore what’s next, from emerging applications to the global race for leadership. 2 min r

Source PDF: all report pdf/Quantum dictionary, part 2_ From potential to progress We go beyond the quantum basics to explore what’s next, from emerging applications to the global race for leadership. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-11-01
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

13037420     1

Transformation 
Quantum dictionary, part 2: From potential to progress 
02 November 2026

Key takeaways 
• In the second and final part of our quantum dictionary series, we move beyond the basics to explore where quantum could go 
next. Today's quantum computers remain limited by noise and errors, but their potential grows rapidly as they scale. Emerging 
applications like quantum AI and sensing could eventually unlock new capabilities and significant economic value. 
• Quantum communication could change both how we connect quantum computers and how we protect information. A quantum 
internet could use entanglement to link quantum systems, creating additional computing power, while quantum networks could 
make eavesdropping detectable and offer new ways to securely transmit information. 
• Meanwhile, the race for quantum leadership is already underway. Governments are pursuing greater quantum sovereignty as the 
technology's potential implications for competitiveness, security and defense grow.

Assessing quantum’s performance and progress 
Quantum advantage versus supremacy 
Quantum advantage is reached when a quantum computer can solve a useful, real-world problem faster than a classical 
computer. The problem is, today’s quantum computers are still too noisy, as we discussed in part one of this series, and qubit 
quality isn’t yet high enough to demonstrate quantum advantage.  
Quantum supremacy takes this one step further. It refers to the point when a quantum computer has computational advantage, 
meaning it can solve a problem that a traditional computer couldn’t in any feasible amount of time.  
Quantum scaling 
Quantum scaling is about building bigger, more powerful quantum computers. It involves overcoming the challenges of 
increasing both the number and the quality of qubits. Today, we’re in the noisy intermediate-scale quantum era. Quantum 
computers have tens to a few hundred qubits, but noise and errors still limit what they can do.1 
However, when quantum computers do scale, they do so exponentially. Every additional qubit doubles the number of operations 
the system can perform. A quantum computer with just 10 qubits could perform more than 100 times the operations of a 
classical computer (Exhibit 1). More qubits could therefore mean more operations and, ultimately, more economic value in the 
industries where quantum computing can be put to work, according to BofA Global Research. 
Exhibit 1: A quantum computer with just 10 qubits could perform more than 100 times the operations than classical 
Number of classical versus quantum operations for a given (qu)bit number (log scale)

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE
